  "content": "\"\"\"本地模拟 NeteaseCloudMusicApi 的 /search 与 /song/detail，供分页自测使用。\n\n真实服务器看不到请求次数，\"滑到底不自动请求\"\"连点不重复请求\"这类断言只能靠这里的请求日志。\n响应结构与真实接口实测一致（baseline-notes 7.3）：\n- 正常页 `{\"result\": {\"songs\": [...], \"hasMore\": bool, \"songCount\": n}, \"code\": 200}`\n- 零结果 / 超出范围 `{\"result\": {\"songCount\": 0}, \"code\": 200}`（没有 songs、hasMore）\n关键字末尾的数字即结果总数，例如 `pga75` 有 75 首，歌名为 `pga75-歌曲1` ~ `pga75-歌曲75`。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\nfrom urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse\n\nPORT = 3999\n\n\nclass MockState:\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.lock = threading.Lock()\n        self.reset()\n\n    def reset(self) -> None:\n        with self.lock:\n            self.requests: list[tuple[str, int | None, int | None]] = []\n            self.delay_first = 0.0          # offset == 0 的响应延时（秒）\n            self.delay_more = 0.0           # offset > 0 的响应延时（秒）\n            self.failures: dict[int, list[str]] = {}  # offset -> 依次消费的失败方式\n            self.empty_pages: set[int] = set()        # 返回空 songs + hasMore=true 的 offset\n\n    def search_offsets(self, keyword: str) -> list[int | None]:\n        with self.lock:\n            return [o for k, _, o in self.requests if k == keyword]\n\n    def search_requests(self, keyword: str) -> list[tuple[str, int | None, int | None]]:\n        with self.lock:\n            return [r for r in self.requests if r[0] == keyword]\n\n\nSTATE = MockState()\n\n\ndef _song(keyword: str, i: int) -> dict:\n    return {\n        \"id\": abs(hash((keyword, i))) % 10_000_000 + 1,\n        \"name\": f\"{keyword}-歌曲{i + 1}\",\n        \"artists\": [{\"id\": 1, \"name\": \"模拟歌手\"}],\n        \"duration\": 200_000,\n        \"album\": {\"id\": 1, \"name\": \"模拟专辑\"},\n    }\n\n\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # noqa: D401 - 静默\n        pass\n\n    def _send_json(self, body: dict) -> None:\n        raw = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n        self.send_response(200)\n        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json; charset=utf-8\")\n        self.send_header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-store\")\n        self.send_header(\"Content-Length\", str(len(raw)))\n        self.end_headers()\n        self.wfile.write(raw)\n\n    def do_GET(self):\n        self._handle()\n\n    def do_POST(self):\n        length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\") or 0)\n        if length:\n            self.rfile.read(length)\n        self._handle()\n\n    def _handle(self) -> None:\n        url = urlparse(self.path)\n        qs = parse_qs(url.query)\n        if url.path == \"/song/detail\":\n            self._send_json({\"songs\": [{\"al\": {\"picUrl\": \"\"}}], \"code\": 200})\n            return\n        if url.path != \"/search\":\n            self._send_json({\"code\": 404})\n            return\n\n        keyword = qs.get(\"keywords\", [\"\"])[0]\n        limit = int(qs[\"limit\"][0]) if \"limit\" in qs else None\n        offset = int(qs[\"offset\"][0]) if \"offset\" in qs else None\n        off = offset or 0\n        with STATE.lock:\n            STATE.requests.append((keyword, limit, offset))\n            delay = STATE.delay_first if off == 0 else STATE.delay_more\n            queue = STATE.failures.get(off) or []\n            failure = queue.pop(0) if queue else None\n            empty_page = off in STATE.empty_pages\n        if delay:\n            time.sleep(delay)\n\n        if failure == \"drop\":       # 网络层失败：不回响应直接断开\n            self.close_connection = True\n            self.connection.shutdown(2)\n            return\n        if failure == \"code400\":    # 业务失败：与真实接口纯空格响应相同\n            self._send_json({\"code\": 400})\n            return\n        if failure == \"noresult\":   # 结构异常：有 code 但没有 result\n            self._send_json({\"code\": 200})\n            return\n\n        if not keyword.strip():\n            self._send_json({\"code\": 400})\n            return\n        m = re.search(r\"(\\d+)$\", keyword)\n        total = int(m.group(1)) if m else 0\n        size = limit or 30\n        if empty_page:\n            self._send_json({\"result\": {\"songs\": [], \"hasMore\": True, \"songCount\": total}, \"code\": 200})\n            return\n        if off >= total:\n            self._send_json({\"result\": {\"songCount\": 0}, \"code\": 200})\n            return\n        end = min(off + size, total)\n        songs = [_song(keyword, i) for i in range(off, end)]\n        self._send_json({\"result\": {\"songs\": songs, \"hasMore\": end < total, \"songCount\": total}, \"code\": 200})\n\n\nclass MockServer:\n    def __init__(self, port: int = PORT) -> None:\n        self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer((\"127.0.0.1\", port), Handler)\n        self.httpd.daemon_threads = True\n        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True)\n\n    def start(self) -> None:\n        self.thread.start()\n\n    def stop(self) -> None:\n        self.httpd.shutdown()\n        self.httpd.server_close()\n",
  "askReview": false,
  "runMode": "auto"
}

### 步骤 1.33 用时 49267 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 22:49:06）

```
I note hash() randomization is fine for in-process use. I'm planning a shared helpers module with a base test class, and setting up the package structure so the test runner can discover and import modules correctly via the project's top-level directory.

Still writing helper setup...

I'm working out how to locate the "more" button on screen by querying component bounds rather than hardcoding coordinates, since the UI layout can shift — filtering buttons by position to find the one in the top-right area.

Then I need to navigate into Settings, toggle on the custom API option if it's off, clear and fill in the URL text field, and save — but I'm being careful since both the menu and the settings page title might share the text "设置", so I need to disambiguate by component type or hierarchy to avoid clicking the wrong element.

For the search helper function, I'm writing logic that clears the search input, types the keyword, and either presses Enter or clicks the search button depending on the enter flag, making sure clear_text runs first so input doesn't get appended to stale text.

For wait_loaded(), I'm waiting for the loading indicator to disappear before requiring the results title to appear. For footer(), I'm checking each of the possible footer state keys (loading more, load more, no more) and returning whichever one is present.

I realize the implicit wait time set to 5s in setUpClass could make these non-existence checks slow since find_component would wait the full timeout each time it doesn't find something — I should set implicit wait to 0 for these faster checks and rely on explicit timeouts elsewhere.

For scroll_to_bottom, I'm looping with up to 20 swipes: checking if the footer is present and returning it, otherwise swiping up within the results list area and waiting briefly between swipes. I'm uncertain whether off-screen cached list items show up in the UI tree before they're actually scrolled into view, so to be safe I'll do one extra swipe after the footer first appears to confirm I've truly reached the bottom.

For scroll_to_top, I'm swiping down repeatedly until I find the first song's exact text match. For visibility checks I'm just looking up components by their text labels, and for toasts I'm starting the listener before the action and then fetching the latest toast with a timeout. Dialogs get closed by finding and clicking the "确定" button, app liveness is checked via `pidof`, and I'm working out the exact hdc commands for setting up and tearing down port forwarding.

I also need to resolve the device serial number properly -- falling back to the driver's own `device_sn` attribute if the class-level one isn't set when tests run standalone. For load-more interactions, I should check for the loading spinner right after clicking since the text gets replaced, and I need to account for card animation timing (roughly 600ms) by adding short waits after clicks. I'm keeping in mind that hdc-based operations each take noticeable time, so with added delays everything becomes observably slower. Now I'm ready to start writing the actual helper functions.

```

\"\"\"一直下滑直到出现 first_text；watch_absent 不为空时，途中每一屏都断言该文本不存在。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if watch_absent is not None:\n                self.assertFalse(self.has_text(watch_absent), f\"滚动途中出现了不应存在的 {watch_absent!r}\")\n            if self.has_text(first_text):\n                self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\", 40)\n                return\n            self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\")\n        self.fail(f\"下滑多次后仍找不到 {first_text!r}\")\n\n    def click_load_more(self) -> None:\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\"))\n\n    def wait_footer_not_loading(self, timeout: float = 20) -> str | None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            f = self.footer()\n            if f != K_LOADING_MORE:\n                return f\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"加载更多的加载指示一直没有消失\")\n\n    def dismiss_error_dialog(self, timeout: float = 8) -> bool:\n        ok = self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"确定\"), timeout=timeout)\n        if ok is None:\n            return False\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"出现了'确定'但不是网络错误弹窗\")\n        self.driver.click(ok)\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        return True\n",
  "askReview": false,
  "runMode": "auto"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE, LOAD_MORE,\n                                  NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n# PART2\n",

### 步骤 1.37 用时 13869 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 22:50:56）

```
I'm worried the exact text match for "60" in test_05 could accidentally match something like a battery percentage display rather than the intended element, so I should consider scoping that assertion more precisely or dropping it to avoid false positives.

Same issue applies to the "31" check in test_06 — instead of a plain text match, I'll verify the element exists and confirm its vertical position aligns with the corresponding song entry, which gives a more reliable check than relying on text content alone.

```

song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.require_text(\"31\", \"追加的第一首序号不是 31\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n# PART3\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n cy = (name.top + name.bottom) // 2\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top <= cy <= r.bottom and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n# PART3\n",

  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        cy = (name.top + name.bottom) // 2\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top <= cy <= r.bottom and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < name.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > name.top - 60 and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE_NAME, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE, LOAD_MORE,\n                                  NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < name.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > name.top - 60 and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE_NAME, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE, LOAD_MORE,\n                                  NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < name.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > name.top - 60 and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n      cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def
