test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < name.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > name.top - 60 and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE_NAME, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",

I'm wondering whether input_text preserves a leading space for the " pgn3" keyword search, and whether title_of will handle that correctly. I also want to check that get_latest_toast works properly in test_14 after start_listen_toast, and whether the keyboard popping up during text entry obscures the search button when pressing back.

{
  "relativePath": "hypium_tests/helpers.py",
  "absolutePath": "/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec99/hypium_tests/helpers.py",
  "resolution": "absolute",
  "content": "\"\"\"cmu-spec99 自测公共部分：应用重启、API 地址切换、搜索与列表滚动辅助。\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport time\n\nfrom hypium import BY, KeyCode\n\nfrom harness.app import restart_app\nfrom harness.driver import HarnessTestCase\n\nBUNDLE = \"cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic\"\nREAL_API = \"http://192.168.1.97:3000\"\n\nLOAD_MORE = \"点击加载更多\"\nNO_MORE = \"没有更多了~\"\nK_INPUT = \"search_input\"\nK_BUTTON = \"search_button\"\nK_LOADING = \"search_loading\"\nK_LIST = \"search_result_list\"\nK_LOAD_MORE = \"search_load_more\"\nK_LOADING_MORE = \"search_loading_more\"\nK_NO_MORE = \"search_no_more\"\nFOOTER_KEYS = (K_LOADING_MORE, K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE)\n\nHDC = shutil.which(\"hdc\") or \"/Applications/DevEco-Studio.app/Contents/sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains/hdc\"\n\n\ndef title_of(keyword: str) -> str:\n    return f'\"{keyword}\" 的搜索结果'\n\n\nclass SearchTestBase(HarnessTestCase):\n    \"\"\"搜索结果分页用例的基类。子类在 setUpClass 里决定连真实服务器还是模拟服务。\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        # 断言\"不存在\"很多，关掉隐式等待，统一用 find_or_none 的显式超时\n        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(0)\n        cls.sn = cls.device_sn or os.environ.get(\"HDC_DEVICE\") or cls.driver.device_sn\n\n    @classmethod\n    def hdc(cls, *args: str) -> str:\n        out = subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", cls.sn, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n        return (out.stdout or \"\") + (out.stderr or \"\")\n\n    # ---------- 应用与设置 ----------\n\n    def restart_to_index(self) -> None:\n        restart_app(self.driver, BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"冷启动后未进入 Index（找不到 search_input）\")\n\n    def app_alive(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(self.driver.shell(f\"pidof {BUNDLE}\").strip())\n\n    def set_api_url(self, url: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Index → 更多 → 设置 → 打开\"自定义API服务器地址\"并填入 url → 返回 Index。不改源代码（U10）。\"\"\"\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"不在 Index，无法进入设置\")\n        top = inp.getBounds().top\n        heads = [b for b in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Button\")) or []) if b.getBounds().bottom < top]\n        self.assertTrue(heads, \"找不到 Index 顶部按钮\")\n        more = max(heads, key=lambda b: b.getBounds().right)\n        self.driver.click(more)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_text(\"设置\", \"更多菜单里没有'设置'\"))\n        label = self.require_text(\"自定义API服务器地址\", \"设置页没有'自定义API服务器地址'\")\n        lb = label.getBounds()\n        cy = (lb.top + lb.bottom) // 2\n        toggles = self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Toggle\")) or []\n        self.assertTrue(toggles, \"设置页找不到 Toggle\")\n        toggle = min(toggles, key=lambda t: abs((t.getBounds().top + t.getBounds().bottom) // 2 - cy))\n        if not self.driver.get_component_property(toggle, \"checked\"):\n            self.driver.click(toggle)\n            self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"打开开关后没有出现地址输入框\")\n        if field.getText() != url:\n            self.driver.clear_text(field)\n            self.driver.input_text(field, url)\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.type(\"Button\").text(\"设置\"), \"找不到'设置'按钮\"))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"地址输入框消失\")\n        self.assertEqual(field.getText(), url, \"API 地址没有设置成功\")\n        for _ in range(3):  # 第一次返回可能只是收起键盘\n            self.driver.press_back()\n            if self.find_or_none(BY.key(K_INPUT), timeout=2) is not None:\n                return\n        self.fail(\"从设置返回后不在 Index\")\n\n    # ---------- 搜索 ----------\n\n    def type_keyword(self, keyword: str) -> None:\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"找不到搜索框 search_input\")\n        self.driver.clear_text(inp)\n        if keyword:\n            self.driver.input_text(inp, keyword)\n        self.driver.wait(0.3)\n\n    def submit(self, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        if enter:\n            self.driver.click(BY.key(K_INPUT))\n            self.driver.press_key(KeyCode.ENTER)\n        else:\n            self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_BUTTON), \"找不到搜索按钮 search_button\"))\n\n    def search(self, keyword: str, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        self.type_keyword(keyword)\n        self.submit(enter)\n        self.require_text(title_of(keyword), f\"没有进入搜索结果页：{keyword}\")\n\n    def wait_loaded(self, timeout: float = 20) -> None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if self.driver.find_component(BY.key(K_LOADING)) is None:\n                return\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"首页全屏加载一直没有结束\")\n\n    # ---------- 列表 ----------\n\n    def exists(self, by) -> bool:\n        return self.driver.find_component(by) is not None\n\n    def has_text(self, text: str) -> bool:\n        return self.exists(BY.text(text))\n\n    def footer(self) -> str | None:\n        for key in FOOTER_KEYS:\n            if self.exists(BY.key(key)):\n                return key\n        return None\n\n    def swipe_list(self, direction: str, distance: int = 80) -> None:\n        self.driver.swipe(direction, distance, area=BY.key(K_LIST), swipe_time=0.25)\n        self.driver.wait(0.6)\n\n    def scroll_to_bottom(self, max_swipes: int = 25) -> str:\n        \"\"\"一直上滑到列表底部出现加载/文案项，返回该项的 key。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if self.footer() is not None:\n                break\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        else:\n            self.fail(\"滑动多次后仍未到达列表底部\")\n        self.swipe_list(\"UP\", 40)  # ### 步骤 1.46 用时 30074 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 22:56:02）

```
I'm planning real server tests against the actual API, checking that search for "想你就写信 李硕 张鑫" returns 40 songs, an empty query returns zero results, and a multi-page query like 周杰伦 triggers load-more behavior past 60 items. Since song names aren't unique, I need to verify counts carefully—confirming that after loading more for the 40-result case, a "no more" indicator appears and the last item index is 40 with nothing beyond it, though the index text might be ambiguous with the status bar.

I'm deciding to fetch actual page data via urllib in the test itself, using the real API's returned song names to match against visible rows rather than relying on index numbers alone, since clock digits in the status bar could coincidentally resemble item indices. I want to add a generalized helper like assert_card(index, name) replacing the earlier index-only assertion, then write test cases: one for the two-page 40-result search verifying scroll-to-load-more behavior and final card matching the last fetched name, another for the zero-result case, following a setup pattern that restarts the app and points it at the real API before each test.

---------- 应用与设置 ----------\n\n    def restart_to_index(self) -> None:\n        restart_app(self.driver, BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"冷启动后未进入 Index（找不到 search_input）\")\n\n    def app_alive(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(self.driver.shell(f\"pidof {BUNDLE}\").strip())\n\n    def set_api_url(self, url: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Index → 更多 → 设置 → 打开\"自定义API服务器地址\"并填入 url → 返回 Index。不改源代码（U10）。\"\"\"\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"不在 Index，无法进入设置\")\n        top = inp.getBounds().top\n        heads = [b for b in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Button\")) or []) if b.getBounds().bottom < top]\n        self.assertTrue(heads, \"找不到 Index 顶部按钮\")\n        more = max(heads, key=lambda b: b.getBounds().right)\n        self.driver.click(more)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_text(\"设置\", \"更多菜单里没有'设置'\"))\n        label = self.require_text(\"自定义API服务器地址\", \"设置页没有'自定义API服务器地址'\")\n        lb = label.getBounds()\n        cy = (lb.top + lb.bottom) // 2\n        toggles = self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Toggle\")) or []\n        self.assertTrue(toggles, \"设置页找不到 Toggle\")\n        toggle = min(toggles, key=lambda t: abs((t.getBounds().top + t.getBounds().bottom) // 2 - cy))\n        if not self.driver.get_component_property(toggle, \"checked\"):\n            self.driver.click(toggle)\n            self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"打开开关后没有出现地址输入框\")\n        if field.getText() != url:\n            self.driver.clear_text(field)\n            self.driver.input_text(field, url)\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.type(\"Button\").text(\"设置\"), \"找不到'设置'按钮\"))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"地址输入框消失\")\n        self.assertEqual(field.getText(), url, \"API 地址没有设置成功\")\n        for _ in range(3):  # ---------- 列表 ----------\n\n    def exists(self, by) -> bool:\n        return self.driver.find_component(by) is not None\n\n    def has_text(self, text: str) -> bool:\n        return self.exists(BY.text(text))\n\n    def footer(self) -> str | None:\n        for key in FOOTER_KEYS:\n            if self.exists(BY.key(key)):\n                return key\n        return None\n\n    def swipe_list(self, direction: str, distance: int = 80) -> None:\n        self.driver.swipe(direction, distance, area=BY.key(K_LIST), swipe_time=0.25)\n        self.driver.wait(0.6)\n\n    def scroll_to_bottom(self, max_swipes: int = 25) -> str:\n        \"\"\"一直上滑到列表底部出现加载/文案项，返回该项的 key。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if self.footer() is not None:\n                break\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        else:\n            self.fail(\"滑动多次后仍未到达列表底部\")\n        self.swipe_list(\"UP\", 40)  # 再推一下，保证底部项完整可见\n        return self.footer()\n\n    def scroll_to_top(self, first_text: str, max_swipes: int = 25, watch_absent: str | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"一直下滑直到出现 first_text；watch_absent 不为空时，途中每一屏都断言该文本不存在。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if watch_absent is not None:\n                self.assertFalse(self.has_text(watch_absent), f\"滚动途中出现了不应存在的 {watch_absent!r}\")\n            if self.has_text(first_text):\n                self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\", 40)\n                return\n            self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\")\n        self.fail(f\"下滑多次后仍找不到 {first_text!r}\")\n\n    def click_load_more(self) -> None:\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\"))\n\n    def wait_footer_not_loading(self, timeout: float = 20) -> str | None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            f = self.footer()\n            if f != K_LOADING_MORE:\n                return f\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"加载更多的加载指示一直没有消失\")\n\n    def dismiss_error_dialog(self, timeout: float = 8) -> bool:\n        ok = self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"确定\"), timeout=timeout)\n        if ok is None:\n            return False\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"出现了'确定'但不是网络错误弹窗\")\n        self.driver.click(ok)\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        return True\n",

**TOOL #78** `write_file` — toolState=executed，accepted=True，id=`toolu_bdrk_01GwS8Z9Dn3njhkeEz28bYpF`

params：
```
{
  "content": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（真实服务器 http://192.168.1.97:3000）：中文关键字、一次加载到底、多页、零结果。\n\n搜索结果会随时间变化，每条用例先直接请求接口核对数据集，不符合时记为 skip（未验证）。\n真实数据里歌名不唯一，用\"序号 + 歌名\"定位卡片。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport urllib.parse\nimport urllib.request\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE, LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase,\n                                  title_of)\n\nR2 = \"想你就写信 李硕 张鑫\"\nR7 = \"周杰伦\"\nR0 = \"qzxwvkjhgfdsa1234567890zz\"\n\n\ndef fetch(keyword: str, offset: int) -> dict:\n    q = urllib.parse.urlencode({\"keywords\": keyword, \"limit\": 30, \"offset\": offset}, quote_via=urllib.parse.quote)\n    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{REAL_API}/search?{q}\", method=\"POST\")\n    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:\n        return json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n\n\nclass SearchPagingRealTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def page(self, keyword: str, offset: int) -> dict:\n        try:\n            return fetch(keyword, offset).get(\"result\") or {}\n        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - 服务器不可达记为前置不满足\n            self.skipTest(f\"前置条件未满足: 真实服务器不可达 {e}\")\n\n    def test_01_two_pages_then_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"场景一~三：R2 首屏 30 首 +'点击加载更多'，点击追加 10 首后显示'没有更多了~'。\"\"\"\n        p1, p2 = self.page(R2, 0), self.page(R2, 30)\n        self.require_precondition(len(p1.get(\"songs\", [])) == 30 and p1.get(\"hasMore\") is True\n                                  and 0 < len(p2.get(\"songs\", [])) < 30 and p2.get(\"hasMore\") is False,\n                                  \"R2 数据集已变化\")\n        total = 30 + len(p2[\"songs\"])\n        self.search(R2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"首屏底部不是'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n        self.assert_card(30, p1[\"songs\"][-1][\"name\"])\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后底部不是'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_card(total, p2[\"songs\"][-1][\"name\"])\n   "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（真实服务器 http://192.168.1.97:3000）：中文关键字、一次加载到底、多页、零结果。\n\n搜索结果会随时间变化，每条用例先直接请求接口核对数据集，不符合时记为 skip（未验证）。\n真实数据里歌名不唯一，用\"序号 + 歌名\"定位卡片。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport urllib.parse\nimport urllib.request\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE, LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase,\n                                  title_of)\n\nR2 = \"想你就写信 李硕 张鑫\"\nR7 = \"周杰伦\"\nR0 = \"qzxwvkjhgfdsa1234567890zz\"\n\n\ndef fetch(keyword: str, offset: int) -> dict:\n    q = urllib.parse.urlencode({\"keywords\": keyword, \"limit\": 30, \"offset\": offset}, quote_via=urllib.parse.quote)\n    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{REAL_API}/search?{q}\", method=\"POST\")\n    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:\n        return json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n\n\nclass SearchPagingRealTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def page(self, keyword: str, offset: int) -> dict:\n        try:\n            return fetch(keyword, offset).get(\"result\") or {}\n        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - 服务器不可达记为前置不满足\n            self.skipTest(f\"前置条件未满足: 真实服务器不可达 {e}\")\n\n    def test_01_two_pages_then_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"场景一~三：R2 首屏 30 首 +'点击加载更多'，点击追加 10 首后显示'没有更多了~'。\"\"\"\n        p1, p2 = self.page(R2, 0), self.page(R2, 30)\n        self.require_precondition(len(p1.get(\"songs\", [])) == 30 and p1.get(\"hasMore\") is True\n                                  and 0 < len(p2.get(\"songs\", [])) < 30 and p2.get(\"hasMore\") is False,\n                                  \"R2 数据集已变化\")\n        total = 30 + len(p2[\"songs\"])\n        self.search(R2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"首屏底部不是'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n        self.assert_card(30, p1[\"songs\"][-1][\"name\"])\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后底部不是'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_card(total, p2[\"songs\"][-1][\"name\"])\n\n    def test_02_multi_page(self):\n        \"\"\"场景二：R7 连续加载，第二页追加在第 31~60 首，底部仍是'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        p1, p2 = self.page(R7, 0), self.page(R7, 30)\n        self.require_precondition(len(p1.get(\"songs\", [])) == 30 and p1.get(\"hasMore\") is True\n                                  and len(p2.get(\"songs\", [])) == 30 and p2.get(\"hasMore\") is True,\n                                  \"R7 数据集已变化\")\n        self.search(R7)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后底部不是'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_card(60, p2[\"songs\"][-1][\"name\"])\n\n    def test_03_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"零结果（U1）：R0 显示'没有更多了~'，不显示'点击加载更多'，不卡在加载中。\"\"\"\n        p = self.page(R0, 0)\n        self.require_precondition(not p.get(\"songs\") and p.get(\"songCount\") == 0, \"R0 不再是零结果\")\n        self.search(R0)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"))\n",

self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        \"\"\"第 i 首卡片左侧的序号文本是 i（同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        name = self.require_text(self.song(kw, i), f\"找不到第 {i} 首\").getBounds()\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(i))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < name.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > name.top - 60 and r.right <= name.left for r in rows),\n                        f\"第 {i} 首的序号不是 {i}\")\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n ### 步骤 1.50 用时 14440 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 23:01:02）

```
I realize the line numbers shifted since my edit. The real issue: after loading more items, the footer gets pushed off-screen by the 30 new items, so it's expected that `footer()` returns None — meaning `wait_footer_not_loading` needs to account for that rather than treating it as a failure.

So I should fix the helper to wait until K_LOADING_MORE disappears, then scroll to the bottom to actually read the footer state — this handles both the success case (footer out of view after append) and the no-more-data case (footer still visible). The UX makes sense: new songs appear below where the user was scrolling, letting them continue browsing, which matches the intended spec. Time to update the helper accordingly.

```

params：

  "old_string": "    def wait_footer_not_loading(self, timeout: float = 20) -> str | None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            f = self.footer()\n            if f != K_LOADING_MORE:\n                return f\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"加载更多的加载指示一直没有消失\")"
}

KeyCode\n\nfrom harness.app import restart_app\nfrom harness.driver import HarnessTestCase\n\nBUNDLE = \"cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic\"\nREAL_API = \"http://192.168.1.97:3000\"\n\nLOAD_MORE = \"点击加载更多\"\nNO_MORE = \"没有更多了~\"\nK_INPUT = \"search_input\"\nK_BUTTON = \"search_button\"\nK_LOADING = \"search_loading\"\nK_LIST = \"search_result_list\"\nK_LOAD_MORE = \"search_load_more\"\nK_LOADING_MORE = \"search_loading_more\"\nK_NO_MORE = \"search_no_more\"\nFOOTER_KEYS = (K_LOADING_MORE, K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE)\n\nHDC = shutil.which(\"hdc\") or \"/Applications/DevEco-Studio.app/Contents/sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains/hdc\"\n\n\ndef title_of(keyword: str) -> str:\n    return f'\"{keyword}\" 的搜索结果'\n\n\nclass SearchTestBase(HarnessTestCase):\n    \"\"\"搜索结果分页用例的基类。子类在 setUpClass 里决定连真实服务器还是模拟服务。\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        # 断言\"不存在\"很多，关掉隐式等待，统一用 find_or_none 的显式超时\n        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(0)\n        cls.sn = cls.device_sn or os.environ.get(\"HDC_DEVICE\") or cls.driver.device_sn\n\n    @classmethod\n    def hdc(cls, *args: str) -> str:\n        out = subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", cls.sn, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n        return (out.stdout or \"\") + (out.stderr or \"\")\n\n    # ---------- 应用与设置 ----------\n\n    def restart_to_index(self) -> None:\n        restart_app(self.driver, BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"冷启动后未进入 Index（找不到 search_input）\")\n\n    def app_alive(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(self.driver.shell(f\"pidof {BUNDLE}\").strip())\n\n    def set_api_url(self, url: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Index → 更多 → 设置 → 打开\"自定义API服务器地址\"并填入 url → 返回 Index。不改源代码（U10）。\"\"\"\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"不在 Index，无法进入设置\")\n        top = inp.getBounds().top\n        heads = [b for b in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Button\")) or []) if b.getBounds().bottom < top]\n        self.assertTrue(heads, \"找不到 Index 顶部按钮\")\n        more = max(heads, key=lambda b: b.getBounds().right)\n        self.driver.click(more)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_text(\"设置\", \"更多菜单里没有'设置'\"))\n        label = self.require_text(\"自定义API服务器地址\", \"设置页没有'自定义API服务器地址'\")\n        lb = label.getBounds()\n        cy = (lb.top + lb.bottom) // 2\n        toggles = self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Toggle\")) or []\n self.driver.get_component_property(toggle, \"checked\"):\n            self.driver.click(toggle)\n            self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", ### 步骤 1.51 用时 12826 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 23:01:15）

```
I realize the assertion checking the list didn't jump back to top becomes meaningless once the footer wait already scrolled to bottom, so I need to restructure test_05: wait for loading_more to disappear without scrolling first, verify song1 is absent and song31 is visible right where the footer was, then scroll afterward. I should add a helper for waiting on loading state.

I'm also checking that scroll_to_bottom behaves correctly while footer is mid-load, and walking through the failure-dialog timing in test_08 and test_10 — making sure swipes during the dialog appearance and the dismiss/assert sequence don't break anything, since swiping on the overlay should just be a no-op.

```

self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        self.assert_card(i, self.song(kw, i))\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n ### 步骤 1.57 用时 35434 ms

**REASON**（2026-10-18 23:08:50）

```
I'm puzzling over test_05's load-more scenario at offset 60 with 15 songs — scroll_to_bottom is returning None after the final swipe, which suggests the footer might be vanishing right after that extra upward swipe at the bottom of the list, possibly because it was only barely visible before the swipe pushed it out of view.

Actually it looks like a race condition: wait_loading_more_gone may be returning before the loading_more indicator even appears in the UI tree, since the click and the UI update aren't perfectly synchronous. That would mean scroll_to_bottom catches load_more or loading_more still present, swipes, and by the time it re-checks the footer, the list has already appended and pushed it off-screen — hence None. The fix should be in load_more_and_wait, waiting for load_more to properly disappear or loading_more to actually show up before proceeding.

Writing helper function logic...

For test_06, I'm checking whether 6 downward swipes of 80% actually lands back on song 31 — doing the math on swipe distance versus item height, it seems plausible the fling could overshoot past song 31 entirely, leaving it below the visible window rather than centered in it.

I think a better fix is to use smaller swipe distances (around 25-30% instead of 80%) with a slower swipe_time to avoid fling overshoot, and parameterize scroll_to_top with a distance argument. Alternatively, Hypium's built-in scroll-to-find using find_component with a scroll_target might handle this more reliably than manual swiping.

```

  "old_string": "    def swipe_list(self, direction: str, distance: int = 80) -> None:\n        self.driver.swipe(direction, distance, area=BY.key(K_LIST), swipe_time=0.25)\n        self.driver.wait(0.6)\n\n    def scroll_to_bottom(self, max_swipes: int = 25) -> str:\n        \"\"\"一直上滑到列表底部出现加载/文案项，返回该项的 key。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if self.footer() is not None:\n                break\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        else:\n            self.fail(\"滑动多次后仍未到达列表底部\")\n        self.swipe_list(\"UP\", 40)  # 再推一下，保证底部项完整可见\n        return self.footer()\n\n    def scroll_to_top(self, first_text: str, max_swipes: int = 25, watch_absent: str | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"一直下滑直到出现 first_text；watch_absent 不为空时，途中每一屏都断言该文本不存在。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if watch_absent is not None:\n                self.assertFalse(self.has_text(watch_absent), f\"滚动途中出现了不应存在的 {watch_absent!r}\")\n            if self.has_text(first_text):\n                self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\", 40)\n                return\n            self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\")\n        self.fail(f\"下滑多次后仍找不到 {first_text!r}\")"
