  "content": "\"\"\"cmu-spec99 自测公共部分：应用重启、API 地址切换、搜索与列表滚动辅助。\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport time\n\nfrom hypium import BY, KeyCode\n\nfrom harness.app import restart_app\nfrom harness.driver import HarnessTestCase\n\nBUNDLE = \"cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic\"\nREAL_API = \"http://192.168.1.97:3000\"\n\nLOAD_MORE = \"点击加载更多\"\nNO_MORE = \"没有更多了~\"\nK_INPUT = \"search_input\"\nK_BUTTON = \"search_button\"\nK_LOADING = \"search_loading\"\nK_LIST = \"search_result_list\"\nK_LOAD_MORE = \"search_load_more\"\nK_LOADING_MORE = \"search_loading_more\"\nK_NO_MORE = \"search_no_more\"\nFOOTER_KEYS = (K_LOADING_MORE, K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE)\n\nHDC = shutil.which(\"hdc\") or \"/Applications/DevEco-Studio.app/Contents/sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains/hdc\"\n\n\ndef title_of(keyword: str) -> str:\n    return f'\"{keyword}\" 的搜索结果'\n\n\nclass SearchTestBase(HarnessTestCase):\n    \"\"\"搜索结果分页用例的基类。子类在 setUpClass 里决定连真实服务器还是模拟服务。\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        # 断言\"不存在\"很多，关掉隐式等待，统一用 find_or_none 的显式超时\n        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(0)\n        cls.sn = cls.device_sn or os.environ.get(\"HDC_DEVICE\") or cls.driver.device_sn\n\n    @classmethod\n    def hdc(cls, *args: str) -> str:\n        out = subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", cls.sn, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n        return (out.stdout or \"\") + (out.stderr or \"\")\n\n    # ---------- 应用与设置 ----------\n\n    def restart_to_index(self) -> None:\n        restart_app(self.driver, BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"冷启动后未进入 Index（找不到 search_input）\")\n\n    def app_alive(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(self.driver.shell(f\"pidof {BUNDLE}\").strip())\n\n    def set_api_url(self, url: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Index → 更多 → 设置 → 打开\"自定义API服务器地址\"并填入 url → 返回 Index。不改源代码（U10）。\"\"\"\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"不在 Index，无法进入设置\")\n        top = inp.getBounds().top\n        heads = [b for b in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Button\")) or []) if b.getBounds().bottom < top]\n        self.assertTrue(heads, \"找不到 Index 顶部按钮\")\n        more = max(heads, key=lambda b: b.getBounds().right)\n        self.driver.click(more)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_text(\"设置\", \"更多菜单里没有'设置'\"))\n        label = self.require_text(\"自定义API服务器地址\", \"设置页没有'自定义API服务器地址'\")\n        lb = label.getBounds()\n        cy = (lb.top + lb.bottom) // 2\n        toggles = self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Toggle\")) or []\n        self.assertTrue(toggles, \"设置页找不到 Toggle\")\n        toggle = min(toggles, key=lambda t: abs((t.getBounds().top + t.getBounds().bottom) // 2 - cy))\n        if not self.driver.get_component_property(toggle, \"checked\"):\n            self.driver.click(toggle)\n            self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"打开开关后没有出现地址输入框\")\n        if field.getText() != url:\n            self.driver.clear_text(field)\n            self.driver.input_text(field, url)\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.type(\"Button\").text(\"设置\"), \"找不到'设置'按钮\"))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"地址输入框消失\")\n        self.assertEqual(field.getText(), url, \"API 地址没有设置成功\")\n        for _ in range(3):  # 第一次返回可能只是收起键盘\n            self.driver.press_back()\n            if self.find_or_none(BY.key(K_INPUT), timeout=2) is not None:\n                return\n        self.fail(\"从设置返回后不在 Index\")\n\n    # ---------- 搜索 ----------\n\n    def type_keyword(self, keyword: str) -> None:\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"找不到搜索框 search_input\")\n        self.driver.clear_text(inp)\n        if keyword:\n            self.driver.input_text(inp, keyword)\n        self.driver.wait(0.3)\n\n    def submit(self, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        if enter:\n            self.driver.click(BY.key(K_INPUT))\n            self.driver.press_key(KeyCode.ENTER)\n        else:\n            self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_BUTTON), \"找不到搜索按钮 search_button\"))\n\n    def search(self, keyword: str, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        self.type_keyword(keyword)\n        self.submit(enter)\n        self.require_text(title_of(keyword), f\"没有进入搜索结果页：{keyword}\")\n\n    def wait_loaded(self, timeout: float = 20) -> None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if self.driver.find_component(BY.key(K_LOADING)) is None:\n                return\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"首页全屏加载一直没有结束\")\n\n    # ---------- 列表 ----------\n\n    def exists(self, by) -> bool:\n        return self.driver.find_component(by) is not None\n\n    def has_text(self, text: str) -> bool:\n        return self.exists(BY.text(text))\n\n    def footer(self) -> str | None:\n        for key in FOOTER_KEYS:\n            if self.exists(BY.key(key)):\n                return key\n        return None\n\n    def swipe_list(self, direction: str, distance: int = 80, swipe_time: float = 0.25) -> None:\n        self.driver.swipe(direction, distance, area=BY.key(K_LIST), swipe_time=swipe_time)\n        self.driver.wait(0.6)\n\n    def scroll_to_bottom(self, max_swipes: int = 25) -> str:\n        \"\"\"一直上滑到列表底部出现加载/文案项，返回该项的 key。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if self.footer() is None:\n                self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n                continue\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\", 40)  # 再推一下，保证底部项完整可见\n            f = self.footer()\n            if f is not None:  # 推的过程中刚好追加完成时，底部项会被挤出屏幕，继续滑\n                return f\n        self.fail(\"滑动多次后仍未到达列表底部\")\n\n    def scroll_to_top(self, first_text: str, max_swipes: int = 25, watch_absent: str | None = None,\n                      distance: int = 80) -> None:\n        \"\"\"一直下滑直到出现 first_text；watch_absent 不为空时，途中每一屏都断言该文本不存在。\"\"\"\n        swipe_time = 0.25 if distance >= 60 else 0.8  # 找中间某首时慢滑，避免惯性滑过头\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if watch_absent is not None:\n                self.assertFalse(self.has_text(watch_absent), f\"滚动途中出现了不应存在的 {watch_absent!r}\")\n            if self.has_text(first_text):\n                return\n            self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\", distance, swipe_time)\n        self.fail(f\"下滑多次后仍找不到 {first_text!r}\")\n\n    def click_load_more(self) -> None:\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\"))\n\n    def assert_card(self, index: int, name: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"序号为 index、歌名为 name 的卡片在当前屏（序号与歌名同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        names = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(name)) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(names, f\"当前屏找不到歌名 {name!r}\")\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(index))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < n.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > n.top - 60 and r.right <= n.left\n                            for n in names for r in rows),\n                        f\"歌名 {name!r} 左侧的序号不是 {index}\")\n\n    def wait_loading_more_gone(self, timeout: float = 20) -> None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if not self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE)):\n                return\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"加载更多的加载指示一直没有消失\")\n\n    def wait_footer_not_loading(self, timeout: float = 20) -> str | None:\n        \"\"\"等加载更多的指示消失，再滑到底返回底部项（追加后原底部项会被新歌曲挤出屏幕）。\"\"\"\n        self.wait_loading_more_gone(timeout)\n        return self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n    def dismiss_error_dialog(self, timeout: float = 8) -> bool:\n        ok = self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"确定\"), timeout=timeout)\n        if ok is None:\n            return False\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"出现了'确定'但不是网络错误弹窗\")\n        self.driver.click(ok)\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        return True\n",

  "content": "\"\"\"cmu-spec99 自测公共部分：应用重启、API 地址切换、搜索与列表滚动辅助。\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport time\n\nfrom hypium import BY, KeyCode\n\nfrom harness.app import restart_app\nfrom harness.driver import HarnessTestCase\n\nBUNDLE = \"cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic\"\nREAL_API = \"http://192.168.1.97:3000\"\n\nLOAD_MORE = \"点击加载更多\"\nNO_MORE = \"没有更多了~\"\nK_INPUT = \"search_input\"\nK_BUTTON = \"search_button\"\nK_LOADING = \"search_loading\"\nK_LIST = \"search_result_list\"\nK_LOAD_MORE = \"search_load_more\"\nK_LOADING_MORE = \"search_loading_more\"\nK_NO_MORE = \"search_no_more\"\nFOOTER_KEYS = (K_LOADING_MORE, K_LOAD_MORE, K_NO_MORE)\n\nHDC = shutil.which(\"hdc\") or \"/Applications/DevEco-Studio.app/Contents/sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains/hdc\"\n\n\ndef title_of(keyword: str) -> str:\n    return f'\"{keyword}\" 的搜索结果'\n\n\nclass SearchTestBase(HarnessTestCase):\n    \"\"\"搜索结果分页用例的基类。子类在 setUpClass 里决定连真实服务器还是模拟服务。\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        # 断言\"不存在\"很多，关掉隐式等待，统一用 find_or_none 的显式超时\n        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(0)\n        cls.sn = cls.device_sn or os.environ.get(\"HDC_DEVICE\") or cls.driver.device_sn\n\n    @classmethod\n    def hdc(cls, *args: str) -> str:\n        out = subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", cls.sn, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n        return (out.stdout or \"\") + (out.stderr or \"\")\n\n    # ---------- 应用与设置 ----------\n\n    def restart_to_index(self) -> None:\n        restart_app(self.driver, BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"冷启动后未进入 Index（找不到 search_input）\")\n\n    def app_alive(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(self.driver.shell(f\"pidof {BUNDLE}\").strip())\n\n    def set_api_url(self, url: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Index → 更多 → 设置 → 打开\"自定义API服务器地址\"并填入 url → 返回 Index。不改源代码（U10）。\"\"\"\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"不在 Index，无法进入设置\")\n        top = inp.getBounds().top\n        heads = [b for b in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Button\")) or []) if b.getBounds().bottom < top]\n        self.assertTrue(heads, \"找不到 Index 顶部按钮\")\n        more = max(heads, key=lambda b: b.getBounds().right)\n        self.driver.click(more)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_text(\"设置\", \"更多菜单里没有'设置'\"))\n        label = self.require_text(\"自定义API服务器地址\", \"设置页没有'自定义API服务器地址'\")\n        lb = label.getBounds()\n        cy = (lb.top + lb.bottom) // 2\n        toggles = self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Toggle\")) or []\n        self.assertTrue(toggles, \"设置页找不到 Toggle\")\n        toggle = min(toggles, key=lambda t: abs((t.getBounds().top + t.getBounds().bottom) // 2 - cy))\n        if not self.driver.get_component_property(toggle, \"checked\"):\n            self.driver.click(toggle)\n            self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"打开开关后没有出现地址输入框\")\n        if field.getText() != url:\n            self.driver.clear_text(field)\n            self.driver.input_text(field, url)\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.type(\"Button\").text(\"设置\"), \"找不到'设置'按钮\"))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        field = self.require_type(\"TextInput\", \"地址输入框消失\")\n        self.assertEqual(field.getText(), url, \"API 地址没有设置成功\")\n        for _ in range(3):  # 第一次返回可能只是收起键盘\n            self.driver.press_back()\n            if self.find_or_none(BY.key(K_INPUT), timeout=2) is not None:\n                return\n        self.fail(\"从设置返回后不在 Index\")\n\n    # ---------- 搜索 ----------\n\n    def type_keyword(self, keyword: str) -> None:\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"找不到搜索框 search_input\")\n        self.driver.clear_text(inp)\n        if keyword:\n            self.driver.input_text(inp, keyword)\n        self.driver.wait(0.3)\n\n    def submit(self, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        if enter:\n            self.driver.click(BY.key(K_INPUT))\n            self.driver.press_key(KeyCode.ENTER)\n        else:\n            self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_BUTTON), \"找不到搜索按钮 search_button\"))\n\n    def search(self, keyword: str, enter: bool = False) -> None:\n        self.type_keyword(keyword)\n        self.submit(enter)\n        self.require_text(title_of(keyword), f\"没有进入搜索结果页：{keyword}\")\n\n    def wait_loaded(self, timeout: float = 20) -> None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if self.driver.find_component(BY.key(K_LOADING)) is None:\n                return\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"首页全屏加载一直没有结束\")\n\n    # ---------- 列表 ----------\n\n    def exists(self, by) -> bool:\n        return self.driver.find_component(by) is not None\n\n    def has_text(self, text: str) -> bool:\n        return self.exists(BY.text(text))\n\n    def footer(self) -> str | None:\n        for key in FOOTER_KEYS:\n            if self.exists(BY.key(key)):\n                return key\n        return None\n\n    def swipe_list(self, direction: str, distance: int = 80, swipe_time: float = 0.25) -> None:\n        self.driver.swipe(direction, distance, area=BY.key(K_LIST), swipe_time=swipe_time)\n        self.driver.wait(0.6)\n\n    def scroll_to_bottom(self, max_swipes: int = 25) -> str:\n        \"\"\"一直上滑到列表底部出现加载/文案项，返回该项的 key。\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if self.footer() is None:\n                self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n                continue\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\", 40)  # 再推一下，保证底部项完整可见\n            f = self.footer()\n            if f is not None:  # 推的过程中刚好追加完成时，底部项会被挤出屏幕，继续滑\n                return f\n        self.fail(\"滑动多次后仍未到达列表底部\")\n\n    def scroll_to_top(self, first_text: str, max_swipes: int = 25, watch_absent: str | None = None,\n                      distance: int = 80) -> None:\n        \"\"\"一直下滑直到出现 first_text；watch_absent 不为空时，途中每一屏都断言该文本不存在。\"\"\"\n        swipe_time = 0.25 if distance >= 60 else 0.8  # 找中间某首时慢滑，避免惯性滑过头\n        for _ in range(max_swipes):\n            if watch_absent is not None:\n                self.assertFalse(self.has_text(watch_absent), f\"滚动途中出现了不应存在的 {watch_absent!r}\")\n            if self.has_text(first_text):\n                return\n            self.swipe_list(\"DOWN\", distance, swipe_time)\n        self.fail(f\"下滑多次后仍找不到 {first_text!r}\")\n\n    def click_load_more(self) -> None:\n        self.driver.click(self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\"))\n\n    def assert_card(self, index: int, name: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"序号为 index、歌名为 name 的卡片在当前屏（序号与歌名同一行，排除状态栏里的同名数字）。\"\"\"\n        names = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(name)) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(names, f\"当前屏找不到歌名 {name!r}\")\n        rows = [c.getBounds() for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(str(index))) or [])]\n        self.assertTrue(any(r.top < n.bottom + 60 and r.bottom > n.top - 60 and r.right <= n.left\n                            for n in names for r in rows),\n                        f\"歌名 {name!r} 左侧的序号不是 {index}\")\n\n    def wait_loading_more_gone(self, timeout: float = 20) -> None:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if not self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE)):\n                return\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.fail(\"加载更多的加载指示一直没有消失\")\n\n    def wait_footer_not_loading(self, timeout: float = 20) -> str | None:\n        \"\"\"等加载更多结束，再滑到底返回底部项（追加后原底部项会被新歌曲挤出屏幕）。\"\"\"\n        # 点击后'点击加载更多'应被加载指示替换；响应很快时可能观察不到，这里只等不断言\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + 3\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline and self.exists(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE)):\n            self.driver.wait(0.2)\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            self.wait_loading_more_gone(timeout)\n            f = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n            if f != K_LOADING_MORE:\n                return f\n        self.fail(\"加载更多一直没有结束\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def dismiss_error_dialog(self, timeout: float = 8) -> bool:\n        ok = self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"确定\"), timeout=timeout)\n        if ok is None:\n            return False\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"出现了'确定'但不是网络错误弹窗\")\n        self.driver.click(ok)\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        return True\n",

  "content": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        self.assert_card(i, self.song(kw, i))\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.wait_loading_more_gone()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 31), \"追加后原底部位置没有出现第 31 首\", timeout=3)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def load_more_and_wait(self) -> str | None:\n        self.click_load_more()\n        return self.wait_footer_not_loading()\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        self.assert_card(i, self.song(kw, i))\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.wait_loading_more_gone()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 31), \"追加后原底部位置没有出现第 31 首\", timeout=3)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=12, distance=25)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",
  "originalContent": "\"\"\"搜索结果手动分页（模拟服务）：请求参数、次数、加载状态、失败、连点、返回/重进/重启、输入边界。\n\n模拟服务跑在本机 127.0.0.1:3999，经 `hdc rport` 映射到模拟器；只通过应用设置切换 API 地址（U10），\n用完在 tearDownClass 里改回真实服务器地址。每条用例用不同的关键字，歌名互不相同。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.helpers import (BUNDLE, K_INPUT, K_LIST, K_LOAD_MORE, K_LOADING, K_LOADING_MORE, K_NO_MORE,\n                                  LOAD_MORE, NO_MORE, REAL_API, SearchTestBase, title_of)\nfrom hypium_tests.mock_server import PORT, STATE, MockServer\n\nMOCK_API = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}\"\n\n\nclass SearchPagingMockTest(SearchTestBase):\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.server = MockServer()\n        cls.server.start()\n        cls.hdc(\"rport\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n        tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n        tc.restart_to_index()\n        tc.set_api_url(MOCK_API)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        try:\n            tc = cls(\"setUp\")\n            tc.restart_to_index()\n            tc.set_api_url(REAL_API)\n        finally:\n            cls.hdc(\"fport\", \"rm\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\", f\"tcp:{PORT}\")\n            cls.server.stop()\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        STATE.reset()\n        self.restart_to_index()\n\n    def song(self, kw: str, i: int) -> str:\n        return f\"{kw}-歌曲{i}\"\n\n    def assert_page_end(self, kw: str, last: int, footer: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"滑到底：最后一首是第 last 首、第 last+1 首不存在、底部项为 footer。\"\"\"\n        got = self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(got, footer, f\"列表底部应为 {footer}，实际 {got}\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last)), f\"底部附近没有第 {last} 首\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, last + 1)), f\"出现了第 {last + 1} 首\")\n        if footer == K_LOAD_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"两种底部文案同时出现\")\n        if footer == K_NO_MORE:\n            self.assertTrue(self.has_text(NO_MORE))\n            self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"没有更多后仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n\n    def assert_index_of(self, kw: str, i: int) -> None:\n        self.assert_card(i, self.song(kw, i))\n\n    # ---------- 场景一 ----------\n\n    def test_01_first_page_shows_load_more_without_auto_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A1 A2 A3 A5 A6：首屏 30 首，滑到底显示'点击加载更多'，滑动不自动请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pga75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首屏没有第 1 首\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0)], \"首页请求应为 limit=30&offset=0 且只有 1 次\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        # 底部继续上拉、停留，都不应触发请求\n        for _ in range(3):\n            self.swipe_list(\"UP\")\n        self.driver.wait(3)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"滑到底后自动发起了请求\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"停留在底部后'点击加载更多'消失\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 31)), \"未点击就追加了第 31 首\")\n\n    def test_02_first_page_loading_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A4：首页请求期间显示全屏加载，不显示任何底部文案。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgb75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页请求期间没有全屏加载 search_loading\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"首页加载结束后没有标题\")\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"首页加载结束后没有歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_03_small_result_shows_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A7：首批即全部（3 首），显示'没有更多了~'，没有'点击加载更多'。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgc3\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0], \"点击'没有更多了~'发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_04_zero_result(self):\n        \"\"\"A8：零结果（响应只有 songCount: 0）显示'没有更多了~'，不当作失败。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgd0\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_NO_MORE), \"零结果时没有'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"零结果时显示了'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"网络请求出错\"), \"零结果被当成网络错误\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    # ---------- 场景二、三 ----------\n\n    def test_05_load_more_appends_until_no_more(self):\n        \"\"\"A9-A15 A18-A21：逐页追加 30→60→75，加载中保留原列表，最后显示'没有更多了~'且不再请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pge75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 2.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING_MORE), \"点击后没有加载指示 search_loading_more\", timeout=2)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(LOAD_MORE), \"加载中仍显示'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"加载中显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)), \"加载更多时出现了全屏加载（替换了列表）\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 30)), \"加载中第一页内容被替换\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_requests(kw), [(kw, 30, 0), (kw, 30, 30)], \"下一页请求应为 offset=30\")\n\n        self.wait_loading_more_gone()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 31), \"追加后原底部位置没有出现第 31 首\", timeout=3)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)), \"追加后列表跳回了顶部\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"第二页后应仍有更多\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 60)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"最后一页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 75, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60])\n\n        # 没有更多后：点击文案、上下滑动都不请求，全程找不到'点击加载更多'\n        self.driver.click(BY.key(K_NO_MORE))\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 1), watch_absent=LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.scroll_to_bottom(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 60], \"没有更多后又发起了请求\")\n\n    def test_06_new_songs_browsable(self):\n        \"\"\"A13 A14：追加的歌曲序号从 31 连续，新卡片可点击打开详情。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgf45\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 45, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.scroll_to_top(self.song(kw, 31), max_swipes=6)\n        self.assert_index_of(kw, 31)\n        self.driver.wait(1)  # 等卡片宽度动画结束\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 33)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击新追加的卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(\"播放\"), \"详情未关闭\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 33)), \"关闭详情后列表位置变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    def test_07_rapid_clicks_single_request(self):\n        \"\"\"A16：快速连点'点击加载更多'只发 1 次请求、只追加 1 页。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgg95\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 1.5\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        b = self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"找不到'点击加载更多'\").getBounds()\n        pos = ((b.left + b.right) // 2, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2)\n        for _ in range(4):\n            self.driver.click(pos)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"连点产生了重复请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertLessEqual(len(self.driver.find_all_components(BY.text(self.song(kw, 60))) or []), 1)\n\n    def test_08_load_more_failure_keeps_list(self):\n        \"\"\"A17：下一页失败（网络断开、业务错误）保留列表、恢复入口，可用相同 offset 重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgh75\"\n        STATE.failures[30] = [\"drop\", \"code400\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"网络失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有出现基线的'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_footer_not_loading(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"业务失败后没有恢复'点击加载更多'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 30, 30], \"重试应使用相同 offset\")\n\n    def test_09_empty_page_stops(self):\n        \"\"\"A22：某页返回空 songs 但 hasMore=true 时视为没有更多，不再出现入口。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgi75\"\n        STATE.empty_pages.add(30)\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_NO_MORE, \"空页后应显示'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30])\n\n    # ---------- 跨场景 ----------\n\n    def test_10_first_page_failure_retry(self):\n        \"\"\"A28：首页失败结束全屏加载，空列表 + '点击加载更多'，点击以 offset=0 重试，可多次重试。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgj75\"\n        STATE.failures[0] = [\"code400\", \"drop\"]\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"首页失败后没有'点击加载更多'重试入口\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"首页失败后没有标题\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(NO_MORE), \"首页失败显示了'没有更多了~'\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assertTrue(self.dismiss_error_dialog(), \"网络失败时没有'网络请求出错'弹窗\")\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOAD_MORE), \"第二次失败后没有重试入口\")\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 1)))\n\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw, 1), \"重试成功后没有第一页歌曲\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0], \"重试应以 offset=0 请求\")\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 0, 0, 30])\n\n    def test_11_back_and_reenter_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A23：加载若干页后返回，再次搜索从 offset=0 开始，列表与 hasMore 不残留。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgk75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        inp = self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.assertEqual(inp.getText(), kw, \"返回后搜索框没有保留关键字\")\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"再次搜索没有进入结果页\")\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_12_background_and_sheet_keep_state(self):\n        \"\"\"A24：切后台再回前台、打开关闭歌曲详情后，列表与分页状态保持，不重发请求。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgl95\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 60, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.driver.go_home()\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n        self.driver.start_app(BUNDLE, wait_time=2)\n        self.require_text(title_of(kw), \"回到前台后不在结果页\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"回到前台后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.has_text(self.song(kw, 60)), \"回到前台后列表变化\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.driver.click(BY.text(self.song(kw, 58)))\n        self.require_text(\"播放\", \"点击卡片没有打开详情\")\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.footer(), K_LOAD_MORE, \"关闭详情后底部状态变化\")\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"回到前台或关闭详情后重发了请求\")\n\n    def test_13_restart_starts_over(self):\n        \"\"\"A25：分页状态不持久化，重启后重新搜索从第一页开始。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgm75\"\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.assertEqual(self.load_more_and_wait(), K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.restart_to_index()\n        self.assertFalse(self.has_text(title_of(kw)), \"重启后没有回到 Index\")\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 30, K_LOAD_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30, 0])\n\n    def test_14_empty_blank_and_enter(self):\n        \"\"\"A26：空输入/纯空格（按钮或回车）toast 且不跳转、不请求；关键字按回车与按钮一致。\"\"\"\n        for text, enter in ((\"\", False), (\"   \", False), (\"\", True), (\"   \", True)):\n            self.type_keyword(text)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            self.submit(enter)\n            toast = self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=3)\n            self.assertEqual(toast, \"请输入搜索内容\", f\"输入 {text!r}（回车={enter}）没有提示\")\n            self.driver.wait(1)\n            self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)), f\"输入 {text!r} 后离开了 Index\")\n            self.assertFalse(self.exists(BY.key(K_LIST)) or self.exists(BY.key(K_LOADING)))\n        self.driver.press_back()  # 收起键盘\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        if not self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)):\n            self.restart_to_index()\n        with STATE.lock:\n            self.assertEqual(STATE.requests, [], \"空输入或纯空格发起了搜索请求\")\n\n        kw = \" pgn3\"  # 带首尾空格的关键字照常提交，不做 trim\n        self.search(kw, enter=True)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.assert_page_end(kw, 3, K_NO_MORE)\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0])\n\n    def test_15_leave_while_loading(self):\n        \"\"\"A27：首页加载中、加载更多中返回，应用不崩溃，Index 可继续使用。\"\"\"\n        kw = \"pgo75\"\n        STATE.delay_more = 3\n        self.search(kw)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.scroll_to_bottom()\n        self.click_load_more()\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"加载更多中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"加载更多中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.exists(BY.key(K_INPUT)))\n        self.assertEqual(STATE.search_offsets(kw), [0, 30], \"离开页面后又发起了请求\")\n\n        kw2 = \"pgp75\"\n        STATE.delay_first = 3\n        self.type_keyword(kw2)\n        self.submit()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_LOADING), \"首页加载中没有全屏加载\", timeout=2.5)\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.require_component(BY.key(K_INPUT), \"首页加载中返回后不在 Index\")\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertTrue(self.app_alive(), \"首页加载中返回后应用崩溃\")\n        STATE.delay_first = 0\n        self.search(kw2)\n        self.wait_loaded()\n        self.require_text(self.song(kw2, 1), \"返回后重新搜索没有结果\")\n",

### 步骤 1.70 用时 16663 ms

**TEXT**

```
搜索结果的手动分页已经做完。clean 构建成功，没有新增告警；HAP 已覆盖安装到 `127.0.0.1:5555`，18 条 Hypium 自测全部通过，没有跳过。

## 代码改动

- [`entry/src/main/ets/api/songApi.ets`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec99/entry/src/main/ets/api/songApi.ets)：`getSongsList(keyword, offset)` 现在带 `limit=30&offset=N`，返回 `{ hasMore, songs }`。
- 是否还有更多只看接口的 `hasMore`，不用 `songCount`。
- `songs` 缺失或这一页为空时，按"没有更多"处理。
- 没有 `result`，或者返回的错误码不是 200，算请求失败。
- [`entry/src/main/ets/common/entities/SongsResult.ets`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec99/entry/src/main/ets/common/entities/SongsResult.ets)：新增 `SearchSongsData`，字段都是可选的。原来的 `SongsData` 没动，所以 `getSongInfo` 不受影响。
- [`entry/src/main/ets/pages/SongsListPage.ets`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec99/entry/src/main/ets/pages/SongsListPage.ets)：沿用 `PlayListPage` 的状态写法（`isLoadingMore`、`hasMore`、`currentOffset`），并按 acceptance 第 2 节的状态机实现。
- 新数据追加到列表后面。
- 加了 `isRequesting` 防重入，快速连点只会发一次请求。
- 无论成功失败都在 `finally` 里恢复 loading 状态。
- 首页失败时，底部显示 `点击加载更多`，点了以 offset=0 重试。
- 列表底部依次是三种状态：加载中的小 SpinKit、`点击加载更多`、`没有更多了~`。
- [`entry/src/main/ets/components/main/SearchComponent.ets`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec99/entry/src/main/ets/components/main/SearchComponent.ets)：搜索按钮和回车（新增 `onSubmit`）走同一段提交逻辑，纯空格和空输入一样只弹 toast `请输入搜索内容`。
- 按验收清单补了 7 个 `.id()`：`search_input`、`search_button`、`search_loading`、`search_result_list`、`search_load_more`、`search_loading_more`、`search_no_more`。

## 自测

- 模拟服务 15 条：用本机 3999 端口的模拟服务加 `hdc rport`，因为本机 3000 端口被 docker 占用。覆盖了请求参数和次数、首页和加载更多的 loading、30→60→75 逐页追加到 `没有更多了~`、零结果、空页、连点、两类失败与重试、返回重进、切后台、重启、空输入/纯空格/回车、加载中离开页面。
- 真实服务器 3 条：中文关键字一次加载到底（30+10 首）、多页加载、零结果。
- 日志在 `logs/build.log`、`logs/install.log`、`logs/selftest.log`、`logs/selftest.json`。
- 跑完后模拟器里的 API 地址已改回 `http://192.168.1.97:3000`，rport 已移除。整个过程没改任何源代码里的地址。
- `git status` 只剩上面 4 个源文件和新增的 `hypium_tests/`，没有构建噪声。`build-profile.json5` 那处修改是会话开始前就有的，不是我改的。

result.output：
````
## Skill: comate-session-ids

**Base directory**: /Users/bress/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids


# 查询当前会话 ID

## 为什么要分两步

引擎 sessionUuid 只能从 hook 日志里拿：每次工具调用**结束后**，日志会写一条带 `conversationId` 和会话 `cwd` 的 PostToolUse 记录。一条命令执行时看不到它自己的记录，所以：

1. 第一步跑一个打时间戳的命令，它结束后日志里就有一条"本会话"的记录；
2. 第二步找"时间戳之后、本工作区的第一条记录"，那就是第一步的记录，ID 一定属于当前会话。

不要改成"取该工作区最近一条记录"：同一工作区开着别的会话时，最近一条往往是别人的，这正是旧版的 bug。用斜杠命令（`/comate-session-ids`）加载本 skill 时不产生工具调用，同样不能依赖"调用 skill 的记录"。

## 步骤

两步必须是**两次独立的工具调用**，不要用 `&&`、`;` 合成一条，也不要并行。

1. 打时间戳：

   ```bash
   python3 ~/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids/scripts/session_ids.py --stamp
   ```

   输出一个整数，比如 `1792229799425`。

2. 紧接着查询（工作区用绝对路径；Agent 的 cwd 可能是 `/`，不要依赖它）：

   ```bash
   python3 ~/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids/scripts/session_ids.py "<工作区绝对路径>" --after <上一步的整数>
   ```

   需要结构化结果时加 `--json`。脚本只读：读 hook 日志，用 `mode=ro` 打开 IDE 状态库，不写任何文件。

3. 按输出回答用户：
   - `engine sessionUuid`：正常情况下 `hook entry` 在时间戳后几秒内，可直接采信。若出现 `WARNING other sessions logged tool calls in the same window`，说明同一时刻别的会话也在调用工具，把候选列给用户；也可以重做一遍两步，通常就不再冲突。
   - `sidebar session ID`：取状态为 `generating`（正在运行）且唯一的那条，附带标题方便用户核对。
   - 一句话说明依据来源（见下文"数据来源"）。

## 结果不完整时

- `engine ... NOT FOUND (no PostToolUse entry ... after the stamp)`：hook 可能写得慢，再执行一次第 2 步（不用重新打戳）。还是没有就如实告诉用户，不要改用其他办法猜。
- `engine ... NOT FOUND (hook log not found)`：本机没有启用这个 hook，告知用户即可。
- `sidebar session ID: UNDETERMINED`：同时有多个或没有 `generating` 会话。把列出的候选（ID、状态、更新时间、标题）给用户，按标题让用户确认，不要自己挑。
- `sidebar ... NOT FOUND`，或候选里没有当前会话：这张表只记录 **Spec 模式**会话，普通 Agent 会话通常没有记录。如实说"未找到 Spec 会话记录"。普通会话的侧边栏 ID 是否等于引擎 sessionUuid **未经验证**，不要说成同一个值。

## 数据来源（供解释与排错）

| 值 | 位置 | 识别方式 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 引擎 sessionUuid | `~/.comate/builtin-hook-state/notify-dispatcher.log`（JSON 行） | 每次工具调用结束后依次写 `start`（含 `event=PostToolUse`、`conversationId`）、`loadWatch`（含会话 `cwd`）、`record`（含 `toolName`，Bash 为 `run_command`）；按 `cwd` 过滤工作区，按时间对齐第 1 步 |
| 侧边栏会话 ID | `<IDE 用户目录>/workspaceStorage/<hash>/state.vscdb`，表 `ItemTable`，key `comateAgentEditor.session.<id>` | `<hash>` 目录下 `workspace.json` 的 `folder` 指向当前工作区；value 是 JSON，含 `sessionId`、`title`、`iconStatus`、`updatedAt` |

IDE 用户目录：macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Comate/User`，Linux `~/.config/Comate/User`，Windows `%APPDATA%\Comate\User`。

不要把 `User/History/` 或 `chatEditingSessions/` 里出现的 ID 当作依据：那是 IDE 的本地历史和编辑快照，可能来自其他会话写过的文件，原文件删掉后快照依然存在。
````
